- **portaljs-add-chart**: Add a chart (line, bar, area, pie, or scatter) to a dataset's showcase in a PortalJS portal. Installs recharts, writes a reusable Chart component, and renders it in the showcase Views section. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-chart.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-dataset**: Add a dataset (CSV, TSV, JSON, or GeoJSON) to an existing PortalJS portal. Appends an entry to datasets.json so the catalog and showcase render it automatically; routes the data by source (local file vs remote URL) — R2 via Git LFS by default, remote URLs by passthrough. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-dataset.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-dcat**: Make a PortalJS portal harvestable by national/EU/US open-data portals — emit standards-compliant DCAT catalog feeds (DCAT 2/3, DCAT-AP, DCAT-US, national profiles) in JSON-LD, Turtle, and RDF/XML at build, with autodiscovery and per-profile conformance checking. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-dcat.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-geo**: Auto-ingest a geospatial file (GeoJSON, Shapefile, GeoPackage, KML/KMZ, FlatGeobuf, CSV-with-geometry) into a PortalJS portal — on your own machine, no server. Normalizes CRS to EPSG:4326, derives a PMTiles render tier + a GeoParquet query tier, pushes all three to R2 via Git LFS, and emits one dual-tier datasets.json entry the showcase renders with &lt;MapPreview&gt; + &lt;GeoQuery&gt;. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-geo.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-map**: Render a GeoJSON dataset on an interactive Leaflet map in the Views section of a dataset's showcase. Installs react-leaflet and a Map component, then renders the map for the chosen dataset. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-map.md) · ★2.4k)
- **portaljs-add-resource**: Add another file (resource) to an EXISTING dataset in a PortalJS portal — a data dictionary, methodology, or an additional data file. Turns a single-file dataset into a multi-resource one; the showcase renders a section per resource. ([datopian/portaljs](https://github.com/datopian/portaljs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/portaljs-add-resource.md) · ★2.4k)
